Native tears the page down within a round trip.\n    var pending = function () { return new Promise(function () {}); };\n\n    var nativeFetch = window.fetch;\n    window.fetch = function (input, init) {\n        var url = (input && input.url) ? input.url : String(input);\n        var method = (init && init.method) || (input && input.method) || \"GET\";\n        post(\"fetch\", url, method, serialize(init && init.body));\n        return pending();\n    };\n\n    var NativeXHR = window.XMLHttpRequest;\n    function InterceptedXHR() {\n        this._method = \"GET\";\n        this._url = \"\";\n    }\n    InterceptedXHR.prototype.open = function (method, url) {\n        this._method = method;\n        this._url = url;\n    };\n    InterceptedXHR.prototype.send = function (body) {\n        post(\"xhr\", this._url, this._method, serialize(body));\n    };\n    InterceptedXHR.prototype.setRequestHeader = function () {};\n    InterceptedXHR.prototype.abort = function () {};\n    InterceptedXHR.prototype.addEventListener = function () {};\n    InterceptedXHR.prototype.removeEventListener = function () {};\n    window.XMLHttpRequest = InterceptedXHR;\n\n    if (navigator.sendBeacon) {\n        navigator.sendBeacon = function (url, data) {\n            post(\"beacon\", url, \"POST\", serialize(data));\n            return true;\n        };\n    }\n    if (window.WebSocket) {\n        window.WebSocket = function (url) {\n            post(\"websocket\", url, \"GET\");\n            throw new Error(\"blocked\");\n        };\n    }\n    if (window.EventSource) {\n        window.EventSource = function (url) {\n            post(\"eventsource\", url, \"GET\");\n            throw new Error(\"blocked\");\n        };\n    }\n    window.open = function (url) {\n        post(\"window.open\", url || \"\", \"GET\");\n        return null;\n    };\n\n    // Serializes a form the way the ACS expects: urlencoded for POST, folded into the\n    // query string for GET, which is where the SDK reads a GET's challenge data from.\n    //\n    // `submitter` is the button that was pressed, or null for `form.submit()`. As in a\n    // browser, its name=value joins the payload and its `formaction`, `formmethod` and\n    // `formenctype` win over the form's. Forms with Submit / Resend / Cancel buttons\n    // are told apart only by that value.\n    var submitForm = function (form, source, submitter) {\n        var override = function (name) {\n            return submitter ? submitter.getAttribute(name) : null;\n        };\n        var method = (override(\"formmethod\") || form.getAttribute(\"method\") || \"GET\").toUpperCase();\n        var action = override(\"formaction\") || form.getAttribute(\"action\");\n        var params = \"\";\n        try {\n            var data = submitter ? new FormData(form, submitter) : new FormData(form);\n            params = new URLSearchParams(data).toString();\n        } catch (e) {\n            console.warn(\"3DS: could not serialize the challenge form\");\n        }\n        var enctype = (override(\"formenctype\") || form.getAttribute(\"enctype\") || \"\").toLowerCase();\n        if (enctype.indexOf(\"multipart\") === 0) {\n            // challengeHTMLDataEntry is urlencoded by spec; send that regardless.\n            console.warn(\"3DS: multipart form encoded as urlencoded\");\n        }\n        if (method === \"POST\") {\n            post(source, action || document.baseURI, \"POST\", params);\n            return;\n        }\n        var url;\n        try {\n            url = new URL(action || document.baseURI, document.baseURI);\n        } catch (e) {\n            // A malformed action is still an attempt: forward the raw string so the SDK\n            // can reject it. Letting this throw would leave a cancelled submit unreported.\n            post(source, action || document.baseURI, \"GET\");\n            return;\n        }\n        url.search = params;\n        post(source, url.href, \"GET\");\n    };\n\n    // Capture phase, so a page's own handler cannot stop this from seeing the event.\n    document.addEventListener(\"submit\", function (event) {\n        event.preventDefault();\n        event.stopPropagation();\n        submitForm(event.target, \"form.submit-event\", event.submitter);\n    }, true);\n\n    // `form.submit()` fires no submit event, and `<body onload=\"forms[0].submit()\">`\n    // is a common ACS pattern — without this override that submission is invisible.\n    // `requestSubmit()` does fire the event, so the listener above covers it.\n    if (window.HTMLFormElement) {\n        HTMLFormElement.prototype.submit = function () {\n            submitForm(this, \"form.submit-call\");\n        };\n    }\n\n    document.addEventListener(\"click\", function (event) {\n        var anchor = event.target && event.target.closest\n            ? event.target.closest(\"a[href]\")\n            : null;\n        if (!anchor) return;\n        var href = anchor.getAttribute(\"href\") || \"\";\n        // In-page links are not network requests. ACS pages use `href=\"#\"` for\n        // expandable sections, and forwarding one would reject the challenge on a\n        // non-EMV3DS URL and burn the single forward the cardholder's answer needs.\n        if (href === \"\" || href.charAt(0) === \"#\") return;\n        // Only http(s) can be the challenge URL. `tel:`, `mailto:` and `javascript:` reach\n        // no network, so they must not spend the forward either.\n        var target;\n        try {\n            target = new URL(href, document.baseURI);\n        } catch (e) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (target.protocol !== \"http:\" && target.protocol !== \"https:\") return;\n        event.preventDefault();\n        post(\"anchor\", href, \"GET\");\n    }, true);\n\n    // Serializes every named control in the document, the way a form would.\n    var collectNamedFields = function () {\n        var params = new URLSearchParams();\n        var controls = document.querySelectorAll(\n            \"input[name], select[name], textarea[name]\"\n        );\n        for (var i = 0; i < controls.length; i++) {\n            var control = controls[i];\n            var type = (control.getAttribute(\"type\") || \"\").toLowerCase();\n            if (control.disabled) continue;\n            // Buttons carry no cardholder input.\n            if (type === \"button\" || type === \"submit\" || type === \"image\" ||\n                type === \"reset\") continue;\n            // An unticked box contributes nothing, same as a real form submission.\n            if ((type === \"checkbox\" || type === \"radio\") && !control.checked) continue;\n            if (control.multiple && control.selectedOptions) {\n                for (var j = 0; j < control.selectedOptions.length; j++) {\n                    params.append(control.name, control.selectedOptions[j].value);\n                }\n                continue;\n            }\n            params.append(control.name, control.value);\n        }\n        return params.toString();\n    };\n\n    // Some issuers send bare markup — named inputs and a button, no <form> — and expect\n    // the client to gather the fields and submit to the challenge URL itself. Nothing in\n    // such a document can produce a request, so there is nothing to intercept and the\n    // button appears dead.\n    //\n    // Bubble phase, deliberately, unlike the handlers above: a page that has its own\n    // mechanism (a form, fetch, XHR, form.submit) reports through that first, and the\n    // forwarder keeps only the first request. So this synthesizes a submit only when\n    // nothing else claimed the interaction.\n    document.addEventListener(\"click\", function (event) {\n        var trigger = event.target && event.target.closest\n            ? event.target.closest(\n                \"button, input[type=submit], input[type=button], input[type=image]\"\n            )\n            : null;\n        if (!trigger) return;\n        // A control inside a form already submits through the listener above.\n        if (trigger.form || document.querySelector(\"form\")) return;\n        post(\"synthesized-submit\", CHALLENGE_URL, \"POST\", collectNamedFields());\n    }, false);\n})();"
